Motivation: 尽管多模态情感分析受限于模态不平衡，但领域仍依赖承诺过高却效果不佳的基于优化的平衡方法。

Method: 提出统一评估框架，在受控设置下测试梯度和基于损失的平衡策略；进行理论诊断，解释这些方法因混淆拟合速度与判别贡献而失败的原因；并提出基于保留集判别性模态估值的研究议程。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI数据集上的实验显示：1) 没有策略可靠地优于晚期拼接（Late Concatenation）；2) 性能对超参数敏感；3) 即使比率校准也无法产生一致的提升。核心问题是损失不等于效用，梯度不等于重要性。

Conclusion: 模态不平衡问题尚未解决，亟需通过基于保留集性能的效用估计来重新审视模态价值。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) remains constrained by modality imbalance, yet the field continues to rely on optimization-based balancing methods that promise more than they deliver. We provide three contributions: 1) a unified evaluation framework testing gradient and loss-based balancing strategies under controlled settings; 2) a theoretical diagnosis explaining why these methods fail, as they conflate fitting speed with discriminative contribution; and 3) a research agenda toward held-out discriminative modality valuation. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI reveal three shortcomings: no strategy reliably outperforms Late Concatenation; performance is sensitive to hyperparameters; and even ratio calibration fails to yield consistent gains. The core issue is fundamental: loss is not utility, and gradients are not importance. Modality imbalance remains unresolved, motivating utility estimation from held-out performance.

</details>


### [110] [Automatic Lyric Transcription for Greek Songs: Scaling and Task Composition Effects in Whisper Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11302)
*Maria Frangiadaki,Dimitrios Damianos,Kosmas Kritsis,Vassilis Katsouros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对希腊语自动歌词转录（ALT）的控制性研究，通过Whisper模型缩放、多任务训练及两阶段语音到歌唱适应，结合自建数据集，显著降低了词错误率，建立了希腊语ALT基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动歌词转录因旋律变化、节奏不规则和伴奏干扰而极具挑战性，特别是在缺乏基准的低资源语言如希腊语中，亟需有效的适配方法和评估基准。

Method: 1. 对Whisper模型进行适应性研究，考察模型缩放效应；2. 通过不同转录-翻译比例的多任务学习优化任务组合；3. 采用两阶段语音到歌唱的适应策略；4. 基于希腊音频数据集（GAD），利用源分离和CTC强制对齐构建段级对齐的歌唱数据集。

Result: 模型缩放一致性地提升了性能；多任务学习主要作为较小容量模型的有益正则化手段；Whisper Large-v3在两阶段适应后达到27.2%的词错误率（WER），相比零样本基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了首个希腊语自动歌词转录基准，证明了Whisper在低资源语言音乐转录中的有效性，为后续相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automatic Lyric Transcription (ALT) remains substantially more challenging than speech recognition due to melodic variability, rhythmic irregularity, and accompaniment interference. This is heightened in low-resource languages like Greek, where no prior benchmark for ALT exists. We present the first controlled study of Whisper adaptation for Greek ALT, investigating model scaling effects, task composition via multitask training in transcribe-translate ratios, and two-stage speech-to-singing adaptation. We also curate a segment-level aligned singing dataset based on the Greek Audio Dataset (GAD) using source separation and CTC forced alignment. Results show that scaling consistently improves performance, while multitask learning acts as a beneficial regularizer primarily for smaller-capacity models. The 2-stage adaptation in Whisper Large-v3 achieves a Word Error Rate (WER) of 27.2%, a significant improvement over zero-shot baselines, establishing the first Greek ALT benchmark.

</details>


### [111] [MultiHuSE: A Multimodal Dataset for Humour Styles and Emotions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11322)
*Mary Ogbuka Kenneth,Foaad Khosmood,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多模态数据集MultiHuSE，包含2,407个视频和1,463个文本样本，涵盖四种心理幽默风格及情感标注。实验表明多模态融合在幽默风格分类上优于单模态（准确率80.1% vs 77.4%），为幽默与情感心理学理论及AI交互研究提供数据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算幽默识别面临挑战，主要局限于二分类任务，且缺乏同时捕捉幽默心理维度与表达变化的数据集。

Method: 构建MultiHuSE多模态数据集，由50名人口统计学多样化的演员表演1,463个文本样本组成，涵盖四种心理幽默风格（亲和、攻击性、自我增强、自我贬低）及中性内容，并附带情感标注；通过基线实验对比单模态与多模态融合方法的性能。

Result: 多模态融合方法在幽默风格分类中准确率达80.1%，优于单模态的77.4%；其中亲和型幽默提升显著（从66%升至74%）。尽管文本信号最强，但融合模型带来了有意义的改进。

Conclusion: MultiHuSE数据集为连接幽默与情感的心理学理论提供了实证支持，并为人类沟通、幸福感及AI驱动交互领域的研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Computational recognition of verbal humour remains a challenging task, requiring an understanding of language, delivery style, emotions, and cultural context. Most existing approaches focus on binary classification and lack datasets that capture psychological dimensions of humour alongside variations in expression. We introduce MultiHuSE, a multimodal dataset comprising 2,407 high-definition videos of 50 demographically diverse actors performing 1,463 text samples across four psychological humour styles (affiliative, aggressive, self-enhancing, and self-deprecating), as well as neutral content. A subset is additionally annotated for underlying emotions. The dataset uniquely captures multiple actor interpretations of the same texts, enabling systematic analysis of expressive diversity. Baseline experiments show that multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal approaches (80.1% vs. 77.4% accuracy) in humour style classification, with particularly strong gains for affiliative humour (66% to 74%). While text provides the strongest individual signal, fusion models deliver meaningful improvements. We hope that MultiHuSE provides empirical support for psychological theories linking humour and emotion, while also opening new avenues for research in human communication, well-being, and AI-driven interaction. The dataset is available for academic use under an End-User Licence Agreement.

</details>


### [112] [E-CONAN (Entailment, CONtradition And Neutral) Benchmarks: Arabic Textual Entailment and Natural Inference Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11334)
*Khloud AL Jallad,Nada Ghneim,Ghaida Rebdawi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了E-CONAN基准，旨在解决阿拉伯语自然语言推理（NLI）资源匮乏的问题。该基准包含两个数据集（E-CONAN-2和E-CONAN-3），由多种来源组成，并用于评估多语言预训练模型、大型语言模型及特定语言模型的零样本分类性能与错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管文本推理在多种语言中取得进展，但阿拉伯语在该领域仍面临资源有限的挑战，缺乏高质量的NLI数据来支持研究与应用。

Method: 构建E-CONAN基准，数据来源包括自动翻译对、人工验证的机器翻译对、基于阿拉伯语作为外语教材的手工构建对以及包含谣言的新闻标题对。利用该基准对9种最先进的多语言预训练模型进行零样本分类评估，并在ArNLI、XNLI和E-CONAN数据集上进行比较；同时评估5个大语言模型（LLMs），引入MARBERT作为阿拉伯语特定基线，并进行详细的定性与定量误差分析。

Result: 实验表明E-CONAN是评估模型泛化能力和微调预训练模型的有价值资源。其多样化的组成提供了比XNLI和ArNLI更广泛且稳健的评估。通过对比展示了阿拉伯语特定模型相对于跨语言方法和基于LLM方法的扩展性能差异，并识别了常见的错误模式。

Conclusion: E-CONAN基准将公开可用，有望丰富阿拉伯语文本蕴含和自然语言推理的研究社区，为相关任务提供更全面的数据支持和评估标准。

Abstract: Natural Language Inference processes pairs of sentences to extract their semantic relations. NLI has been a hot research topic, integrated as a main component in other NLP applications. Despite significant advancements in textual inference across various languages all around the world, Arabic language still suffers from limited resources in this domain. To address this gap, this paper introduces E-CONAN benchmarks that are composed of sentences pairs from various sources: (1) automatically-translated pairs, (2) human-validated machine-translated pairs, (3) hand-crafted pairs from teaching Arabic as foreign language books, and (4) headlines pairs from different news channels containing rumors. E-CONAN contains two benchmark datasets, E-CONAN-2, a 2-way dataset (RTE) and E-CONAN-3, a 3-way dataset (NLI). Additionally, we have used E-CONAN benchmarks to evaluate 9 state-of-the-art multilingual pretrained models using zero-shot classification. Models were evaluated across the ArNLI, XNLI, and E-CONAN datasets. Results show that E-CONAN is a potentially valuable resource for evaluating model generalization and even for fine-tuning pre-trained models. Its diverse composition, derived from a combination of sources, offers a broader and more robust assessment compared to XNLI and ArNLI. In addition, we have evaluated 5 LLMs on E-CONAN-3 dataset. Moreover, we incorporated MARBERT as a representative Arabic-specific baseline and conducted performance evaluation comparison to demonstrate how Arabic-specific models scale against cross-lingual and LLM-based approaches on the E-CONAN benchmarks. Furthermore, we conducted detailed qualitative and quantitative error analysis to analyze frequent error patterns. E-CONAN benchmarks will be publicly available, we hope that it will enrich research community in Arabic textual entailment and natural language inference.

</details>


### [113] [On the Impact of Anonymization on the Performance of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11335)
*Tobias Deußer,Max Hahnbück,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Tobias Uelwer,Christian Bauckhage,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了输入数据匿名化对大型语言模型性能的影响，发现匿名化虽普遍降低性能，但效果因模型能力和任务类型而异。高能力模型依赖实体信息更重，性能下降更大；部分任务（如TruthfulQA）甚至受益，而检索类任务受损严重。可逆匿名化优于不可逆删除，且显式提示无效。结论是匿名化需与模型和任务协同设计，以平衡隐私与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在敏感领域部署，保护个人身份信息成为关键实践，但匿名化对模型实用性的具体影响尚不明确，亟需系统性实证研究来理解隐私与性能之间的权衡。

Method: 对五个主流语言模型在十一个多样化基准测试上进行系统性实证评估，比较原始输入与伪名化输入下的性能差异；进一步实验对比了可逆与不可逆匿名化技术的效果，并测试了显式提示模型关于匿名化的影响。

Result: 1. 匿名化总体导致性能下降，但高度细微：高能力模型（如Qwen2.5-72B、GPT-4o mini）性能降幅最大，显示其对特定实体信息的更强依赖；2. 任务依赖性显著：TruthfulQA任务性能提升，而RGB等检索任务出现灾难性下降；3. 可逆匿名化（保留实体唯一性）显著优于不可逆方法（如文本删除）；4. 显式提示模型注意匿名化无显著益处。

Conclusion: 匿名化并非通用解决方案，必须根据具体模型和能力及目标任务进行协同设计，才能有效平衡隐私保护与模型效用。研究结果为构建更健壮、隐私感知的人工智能系统提供了重要基线。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains, anonymizing input data to protect personally identifiable information has become a critical practice. However, the impact of this anonymization on model utility is not well understood. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of the trade-off between privacy and performance. We evaluate five prominent language models across eleven diverse benchmarks, comparing their performance on original versus pseudonymized inputs. Our results reveal that while anonymization generally degrades performance, the effect is highly nuanced. We find that more capable models, such as Qwen2.5-72B and GPT-4o mini, suffer the largest performance drops, suggesting a stronger reliance on specific entity information. The impact is also task-dependent: performance on TruthfulQA improves with anonymization, while retrieval-focused tasks like RGB experience a catastrophic decline. Further experiments show that reversible anonymization techniques that preserve entity uniqueness significantly outperform irreversible ones like redaction, and that explicitly prompting models about anonymization offers no discernible benefit. We conclude that anonymization is not a one-size-fits-all solution and must be co-designed with the model and task in mind to balance privacy and utility effectively. Our findings provide a crucial baseline for developing more robust, privacy-aware AI systems.

</details>


### [114] [SEAR: Segment-Evidence-Aware Routing for Weak-to-Strong Multilingual Speech MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11355)
*Huy Hoang Le,Long-Bao Nguyen,Minh Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了参与MLC-SLM挑战赛第2任务的系统，通过适配Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct模型，结合基于片段证据感知的数据与后训练流程，生成多语言音频选择题数据。系统利用文本探针区分弱（仅文本可答）和强（依赖音频）样本，分别进行监督微调和强化学习，最终在官方评估集上达到90.92%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge中的任务2，提升多语言对话语音语言模型在处理复杂音频语义理解及问答任务上的性能，特别是针对跨语言和口音变体的鲁棒性。

Method: 1. **数据预处理**：将带时间戳的ASR转录转换为连贯的事件跨度，扩展边界后从原始录音中裁剪。
2. **数据合成**：使用Qwen3.6-27B合成互补语义多项选择题(MCQs)，使用Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite合成声学MCQs。
3. **质量控制**：执行结构、接地、答案一致性及目标模型可训练性检查，最终获得359,825个经过验证的MCQs，涵盖21种语言和口音变体。
4. **数据分层与训练**：
   - 使用纯文本探针将数据分为“弱”项（仅文本可答）用于监督微调(SFT)。
   - “强”项（依赖音频）用于基于组序列策略优化(GSPO)的强化学习(RL)。
   - RL过程通过去偏优势估计、序列级重要性校正和动态过滤进行稳定。

Result: 系统在最终的官方评估集上取得了90.92%的准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种有效的多模态数据合成与分层训练策略，通过结合大语言模型和专用声学模型生成高质量、多样化的多语言音频问答数据，并利用混合训练范式（SFT+RL），显著提升了多语言对话语音语言模型的性能。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for Task~2 of the second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge. We adapt Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct with a segment-evidence-aware data and post-training pipeline. A language model converts timestamped ASR into coherent event spans, which are expanded by a boundary margin and cropped from the original recording. We then synthesize complementary semantic MCQs with Qwen3.6-27B and acoustic MCQs with Gemini~3.1 Flash-Lite, followed by structural, grounding, answer-consistency, and target-model trainability checks, yielding 359,825 verified MCQs across 21 language and accent variants. A text-only probe partitions the data into weak, text-answerable items used for supervised fine-tuning and strong, audio-dependent items used for reinforcement learning with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), stabilized by debiased advantages, sequence-level importance correction, and dynamic filtering. Our system obtains 90.92% accuracy on the final official evaluation set.

</details>


### [115] [TransClean: A Benchmark for Detecting and Extracting Clean Translations from Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11399)
*Shenbin Qian,Yves Scherrer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型（LLM）机器翻译中普遍存在的“翻译噪声”问题，本文构建了首个系统性基准TransClean，分析了12种噪声模式，并评估了两种提取方法以提升翻译输出的纯净度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM广泛用于机器翻译，但其输出常包含语言标签、解释或双语重复等无关文本（即翻译噪声）。目前缺乏对该问题的专用基准和系统性研究。

Method: 1. 分析79万+条来自12个LLM的22种语言对翻译输出，识别出12种常见的格式和内容噪声模式；2. 构建TransClean基准，包含9,900对（8,800合成+1,100人工标注）噪声与干净译文实例；3. 评估两种提取方法：基于质量估计模型的跨度提取法和基于LLM提示的提取法。

Result: 成功识别并归类了12种噪声模式；建立了包含近万实例的高质量基准TransClean；验证了所提两种提取方法在去除噪声方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了首个系统框架来评估和改进LLM翻译输出的整洁性，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for machine translation, yet their outputs often contain additional text beyond the translation itself, such as language labels, explanations or bilingual repetitions, which we term translation noise. Despite its prevalence, this problem lacks dedicated benchmarks and systematic study. We analyze over 790,000 translation outputs from 12 LLMs across 22 language pairs (LPs) and identify 12 recurring noise patterns, which we group into formatting and content noise. Building on the observed patterns, we construct TransClean, a controlled benchmark of 9,900 pairs of noisy and clean translation outputs, comprising 8,800 synthetically generated instances and 1,100 manually curated authentic instances. We evaluate two extraction approaches on the TransClean benchmark: 1) a span-based extraction method leveraging translation quality estimation models for span detection, and 2) an LLM-based extraction method that prompts an LLM to isolate the translation. Our benchmark and analysis provide the first systematic framework to evaluate and improve the cleanliness of LLM translation outputs.

</details>


### [116] [Cross-Lingual Clinical Annotation Projection as Constrained Text Generation: A Six-Language Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11450)
*Álvaro Rey-Blanes,Francisco J. Moreno-Barea,Francisco J. Veredas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究证实，基于约束大语言模型（LLM）的直接投影方法在跨语言临床注释转移中表现最优。通过GLM 5.2和Gemma4:31B等模型，实现了比现有最先进水平高出0.0564-0.1512的严格F1分数，为构建多语言临床语料库提供了高效、低成本且可验证的技术路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定跨语言临床注释投影是否可以被构建为一个保持文本完整性的文档级生成任务，以产生可验证的字符级注释，并评估其相对于候选集投影管道的鲁棒性和计算权衡。

Method: 开发了一种受限LLM投影工作流，将实体标签直接插入不可变的目标语言文本中，随后进行确定性验证和字符偏移量重建。将该方法与监督式候选跨度投影及混合ML-LLM refinement进行比较，用于将西班牙语的疾病、症状和程序注释转移到六种语言中。使用MultiClinAI金标准进行评估，采用严格的跨度匹配和字符重叠F1指标。

Result: 直接LLM投影取得了最强且最一致的性能。GLM 5.2在18种语言-实体组合中平均严格F1得分为0.9201，本地部署的Gemma4:31B达到0.9133。最佳LLM配置在所有18种设置下均优于先前的最先进水平，提升了0.0564-0.1512的Strict F1，生成了55,416个具有重建偏移量的接地提及（grounded mentions）。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的直接投影能够实现高质量的跨语言临床注释转移，为扩展临床NLP资源至缺乏标注数据集和专用工具的语言提供了一种实用方法。结合本地推理和确定性验证，该方法能显著减少专家时间和成本，加速多语言临床语料库的建设。

Abstract: Background: To determine whether cross-lingual clinical annotation projection can be formulated as a text-preserving, document-level generative task that produces verifiable character-level annotations for multilingual clinical corpus construction, and to characterize its robustness and computational trade-offs relative to candidate-based projection pipelines. Methods: We developed a constrained LLM projection workflow that inserts entity tags directly into immutable target-language text, followed by deterministic validation and character-offset reconstruction. We evaluated it alongside supervised candidate-span projection and hybrid ML-LLM refinement for transferring Spanish Disease, Symptom, and Procedure annotations into six languages. Evaluation used MultiClinAI gold standard with strict span matching and character-overlap F1 Results: Direct LLM projection achieved the strongest and most consistent performance. GLM 5.2 obtained a mean Strict F1 of 0.9201 across 18 language-entity combinations, while locally deployable Gemma4:31B achieved 0.9133. The best LLM configuration improved Strict F1 over the previous state of the art in all 18 settings, by 0.0564-0.1512, yielding 55,416 grounded mentions with reconstructed offsets. Conclusions: Direct LLM-based projection enables high-quality multilingual clinical annotation transfer and provides a practical approach for extending clinical NLP resources to languages with fewer annotated datasets and language-specific tools. Combined with local inference and deterministic validation, it can substantially reduce expert time and cost for multilingual clinical corpus construction.

</details>


### [117] [Structural priors for data-efficient language learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11505)
*Yana Veitsman,Jonas Mayer Martins,Jonathan Lautenschlager,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过非语言数据（如音乐、概率语法）进行结构迁移以优化多语言模型初始化，发现虽能降低训练损失并减少权重变化，但未能显著提升下游语言任务性能，且效率低于直接增加语言数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在减少自然语言处理对大规模数据和计算资源的依赖，探索利用非语言数据诱导先验知识以辅助语言建模的方法。

Method: 采用“结构迁移”策略，首先使用非语言数据（音乐、概率语法、细胞自动机等）训练模型以形成有益的权重初始化，随后在自然语言数据上进行微调；通过下一词预测损失、权重变化幅度及下游语言基准测试进行评估。

Result: 使用特定符号数据（音乐、概率语法、细胞自动机）进行预训练可获得比随机初始化更低的语言建模损失，并伴随较小的后续语言训练权重偏移，表明其定位了更有利的参数空间区域；然而，较低的损失并未一致转化为更好的下游语言性能，且非语言数据迁移的效率低于直接使用额外语言数据。

Conclusion: 非语言数据可作为语言数据在“下一词预测”目标上的部分替代方案，但无法可靠地支持更广泛的语言学泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient language learning requires methods to reduce the reliance on large data and computational resources. We investigate structural transfer: First training models on non-language data to induce useful priors for natural language. This approach is a form of weight initialization for multilingual language modeling. We evaluate transfer via next-token-prediction loss, weight shifts in the model, and downstream linguistic benchmarks. Several symbolic data types - notably music, probabilistic grammars, and cellular automata - yield lower language-modeling loss than random initialization. These gains coincide with smaller weight shifts during subsequent language training, suggesting that structural transfer positions models in a more favorable region of the parameter space. However, a lower loss does not translate consistently into better downstream linguistic performance, and transfer from non-language data is less efficient than additional language data. We conclude that non-language data can serve as a partial substitute for language data for the training objective of next-token prediction but does not reliably support broader linguistic generalization.

</details>


### [118] [Complex-Text Robustness Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Low-Resource Multilingual Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11545)
*Tianlun Zuo,Ziyu Zhang,Tingzhi Mao,Zhonghua Fu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出低资源多语言TTS复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架，包含三维评估指标及轻量级Text Risk Score (TRS)，揭示常规测试未覆盖的系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言TTS评估主要关注自然度、说话人相似性和内容一致性，缺乏对数字、日期、命名实体、长句、代码切换等复杂输入下系统鲁棒性的深入诊断。

Method: 构建涵盖内容一致性、语言一致性和生成稳定性的三维鲁棒性评估体系；针对泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语和印尼语设计多语言测试方案；引入字符错误率、语言识别准确率和时长异常率等自动诊断指标；提出无需人工标注或模型训练的轻量级Text Risk Score (TRS)以预测合成风险。

Result: 在OmniVoice、VoxCPM2和MMS-TTS上的实验表明，复杂文本暴露出常规短句子评估未能反映的系统性失败模式；不同系统在数字归一化、命名实体处理、长文本生成和代码切换方面存在显著差异的脆弱性；TRS与内容错误及时长异常呈正相关，证实了其作为低成本预合成风险指示器的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效诊断低资源多语言TTS在处理复杂文本时的系统性缺陷，TRS为预合成阶段的风险评估提供了实用且低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-resource multilingual text-to-speech (TTS) systems have expanded language coverage, but their robustness under complex text inputs remains insufficiently diagnosed. Existing evaluations mainly focus on naturalness, speaker similarity, and content consistency using regular test sentences, while providing limited insight into how multilingual TTS systems fail when handling challenging inputs such as numbers, dates, named entities, long sentences, code-switched expressions, and punctuation-related structures. This paper proposes a complex-text robustness diagnosis framework for low-resource multilingual TTS. We evaluate robustness from three dimensions: content consistency, language consistency, and generation stability. A multilingual robustness testing scheme is designed for Thai, Vietnamese, Swahili, and Indonesian, covering ordinary sentences and multiple types of complex text inputs. We further introduce automatic diagnostic metrics, including character error rate, language identification accuracy, and duration abnormal rate. To support input-level risk analysis before speech generation, we propose a lightweight Text Risk Score (TRS), which estimates synthesis risk from interpretable text features without manual annotation or model training. Experiments on three representative multilingual TTS systems, including OmniVoice, VoxCPM2, and MMS-TTS, show that complex text inputs expose systematic failure patterns that are not fully reflected by ordinary short-sentence evaluation. Different systems exhibit distinct vulnerabilities in number normalization, named entity handling, long-text generation, and code-switched input processing. Furthermore, TRS shows a positive correlation with content errors and duration abnormalities, demonstrating its usefulness as a low-cost pre-synthesis indicator for complex-text risk diagnosis in low-resource multilingual TTS.

</details>


### [119] [A Training-Free, Alignment-Free Approach to Corporate Intelligence: Application to SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11620)
*Jean-François Delpech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于确定性稀疏种子向量的无训练、无对齐框架，用于金融披露分析。通过将词字符串哈希到固定高维基中，实现文档的线性组合和快速比较，无需LLM或GPU硬件，即可在SEC文件等大规模语料库中提取可解释的语义特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维稠密文本嵌入和大语言模型在金融披露分析中面临的上下文窗口限制、幻觉风险、高计算成本以及不同独立训练模型间向量空间旋转不一致等问题。

Method: 构建一个基于确定性稀疏种子向量的框架。将词字符串哈希到固定的高维基中，使所有文档和时间段处于共同的坐标系中。通过在线性组合句子上下文的种子向量，生成特定于语料库的语义签名，支持文档比较、发行人指纹识别和主题句子提取。

Result: 在多年度SEC文件（10-K, 10-Q, 8-K）语料库上验证了该方法的有效性。成功识别出波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断和本吉收购Viterra等重大企业事件，形成独特且可解释的语义轮廓，并能追溯至产生这些轮廓的具体源句子。整个过程无需领域特定训练或LLM推理，且在普通CPU硬件上实现亚秒级处理。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种高效、可解释且低成本的替代方案，用于从金融披露文件中提取结构化信息，克服了现有深度学习方法的局限性。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [120] [Structured Transforms for Low-Overhead Quantization of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11687)
*Daria Cherniuk,Alexander Rudikov,Boris Kashin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Kashin分解的改进权重量化算法，利用符号随机化离散余弦变换（DCT）替代密集随机正交矩阵，将迭代成本从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，并结合贪婪交替更新算法和预训练误差补偿，实现了在OPT、Llama-2等模型上4-bit量化的稳定且高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Kashin分解的大语言模型权重量化方法存在计算成本高（O(N²)）、依赖多重启k-means导致收敛瓶颈，以及在某些压力配置下数值不稳定（如发散或NaN错误）的问题。

Method: 1. 使用符号随机化的离散余弦变换（DCT）替代密集随机正交矩阵，实现结构化且高效的正交变换。
2. 采用带有交替更新的贪婪算法，确保因子具有四峰分布以支持稳定的2-bit聚类，并提供聚类中心的闭式初始化，消除多重启k-means瓶颈。
3. 结合OPTQ风格的顺序误差补偿和QuIP风格的不相干预处理，构建JAX量化流水线。

Result: 在OPT、Llama-2和Pythia模型上，该方法在每通道4-bit量化下与OPTQ、QuIP、QuIP-RG及QuIP#变体性能相当，且具有更好的墙钟时间扩展性。相比QuIP变体易出现高困惑度或数值崩溃的情况，Kashin-DCT保持数值稳定，接近FP16基线。推理时每个权重分解为两个适合原生2-bit硬件的2-bit因子代码。

Conclusion: 该算法通过引入结构化正交变换和优化聚类策略，显著提升了Kashin分解量化的计算效率和数值稳定性，同时保持了高精度性能，特别适合原生2-bit硬件加速推理。

Abstract: We revisit Kashin-decomposition-based weight quantization for large language models and propose an improved algorithm with stronger convergence properties and structured, efficient orthogonal transforms. The method retains the core factorization of each weight into two components -- one with bounded infinity norm and the other with bounded infinity norm after an orthogonal transformation -- but replaces the dense random orthogonal matrix with a sign-randomized Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), reducing the per-iteration cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$. The proposed greedy algorithm with alternating updates guarantees the four-peak distribution required for stable 2-bit clustering of each factor and admits closed-form initialization of cluster centers, removing the multi-restart k-means bottleneck of prior work. Composed with OPTQ-style sequential error compensation and QuIP-style incoherence preprocessing, the resulting JAX pipeline is competitive with OPTQ, QuIP, QuIP-RG and a fine-tuning- and vector-quantization-free variant of QuIP# at 4-bit per channel on OPT, Llama-2 and Pythia, with favorable wall-clock scaling. The bounded-$\ell_\infty$ factorization is also notably robust: on stress configurations where QuIP variants diverge to four-digit perplexity (Pythia-6.9B) or abort with NaNs in LDL back-substitution (Mistral-7B), Kashin-DCT remains numerically stable and stays close to FP16 baseline. At inference time, each weight decomposes into two 2-bit factor codes per channel that are structurally suited to native-2-bit hardware.

</details>


### [121] [Negative Self-Distillation: Learning to Reason by Avoiding Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11699)
*Rongcan Pei,Zhepei Wei,Shuyao Xu,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出负向自蒸馏（NSD）框架，通过让模型远离自身生成的错误推理轨迹来优化LLM，解决传统On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 在复杂推理任务中因过度模仿“特权信息”而导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD虽然流行，但会抑制模型的不确定性表达和探索性自我纠正行为，导致在复杂推理任务上性能严重退化。

Method: 1. NSD框架：利用模型生成特定问题的负面条件（如“粗心的推理者”），并推动学生分布远离该负面教师；2. 动态门控机制：自动识别并隔离推理关键token，避免对基础语言token的无差别惩罚，从而保护语言先验能力。

Result: 实验表明，NSD一致优于OPSD及其他无标签、自引导的强化学习（RL）基线方法。

Conclusion: NSD是一种有效的LLM自改进范式，通过避免模仿有缺陷的推理并保护语言能力，显著提升了复杂推理任务的表现。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a popular paradigm for large language model (LLM) self-improvement, allowing models to act as their own teachers by leveraging privileged information such as ground-truth solutions. However, recent findings indicate that OPSD can severely degrade the performance of LLMs on complex reasoning tasks: By forcing the student to imitate an artificially confident reasoning trace conditioned on privileged information, OPSD inadvertently suppresses expressions of uncertainty and penalizes the exploratory, self-corrective behaviors required to solve challenging problems. To address this, we introduce Negative Self-Distillation (NSD), a new framework that optimizes LLMs by diverging from flawed reasoning rather than imitating privileged solutions. Instead of relying on ground-truth answers or external supervision, NSD uses the model itself to generate a question-specific negative condition (eg, acting as a ``careless reasoner'') and pushes the student's distribution away from this self-generated negative teacher. Naively applying unlearning objectives to achieve this divergence is problematic, as flawed reasoning tokens are confounded with basic linguistic tokens; indiscriminately penalizing both risks catastrophically degrading the model's foundational language capabilities. We resolve this by designing a dynamic gating mechanism that automatically identifies and isolates reasoning-critical tokens, ensuring gradient updates target only behavioral flaws while preserving the model's linguistic priors. Empirically, NSD consistently outperforms OPSD and other label-free, self-bootstrapping reinforcement learning (RL) baselines.

</details>


### [122] [The Eloquence submission for Task 2 of the Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11724)
*Jordi Luque,Lorenzo Concina,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Eloquence团队在Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM挑战赛中，针对21种语言的MCQA任务提出了三种方法：基于LoRA微调Voxtral-Mini-3B（准确率0.72）、对冻结的Voxtral-24B应用多模态上下文学习以纠正标签偏差（最佳准确率0.81），以及基于三层语音锚定记忆免训练检索系统（准确率0.68）。所有方法均显著优于官方基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MLC-SLM挑战赛Task 2中的多语言多项选择题问答（MCQA）问题，涉及21种语言，旨在探索不同技术路径以提升跨语言音频理解与问答性能。

Method: 1. 使用交叉语言数据增强、ASR转录增强和时间戳感知音频裁剪，通过LoRA微调Voxtral-Mini-3B模型；2. 对冻结的Voxtral-24B模型应用多模态上下文学习（ICL）以校正强标签偏差；3. 构建基于声学身份、语义内容和知识图谱的三层语音锚定记忆的免训练检索系统。

Result: 第一种方法在评估第二阶段达到0.72的宏准确率；第二种方法达到0.81的最佳结果；第三种方法达到0.68的准确率。三种系统均显著优于官方基线。

Conclusion: 通过微调、多模态ICL和免训练检索三种策略，有效提升了多语言MCQA任务的性能，其中基于ICL的方法效果最佳，证明了其在处理标签偏差方面的优势。

Abstract: This paper details the Eloquence team's approach to Task 2 of the 2nd MLC-SLM challenge at Interspeech 2026, which involves multilingual Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) across 21 languages. Three approaches are explored. First, we fine-tune Voxtral-Mini-3B via LoRA with cross-lingual data augmentation, ASR transcript augmentation and timestamp-aware audio cropping, achieving 0.72 macro-accuracy on evaluation Phase 2. Second, we apply multimodal in-context learning (ICL) to the frozen Voxtral-24B model to correct a strong label bias, reaching 0.81, our best result. Third, a training-free retrieval system based on a three-layer voice-anchored memory combining acoustic identity, semantic content, and a knowledge graph achieves 0.68. All three systems substantially outperform the official baseline.

</details>


### [123] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LOCUS方法，通过任务感知低秩适应子空间在保持偏好对齐效果的同时显著减少LLM输出长度和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务成本与输出序列长度成正比，而标准偏好对齐往往增加响应冗余度却不提升效用。

Method: LOCUS选择任务感知的低秩适应子空间以最小化输出Token成本，同时受限于效用约束；在此子空间内，后训练保留原生偏好目标且骨干网络冻结。

Result: 在Anthropic HH-RLHF对话偏好上，Pythia-2.8B续写长度减少最高39.84%，Qwen2.5-3B减少14.87--17.58%；仅更新0.24--0.28%参数，内部偏好诊断无显著变化。

Conclusion: 参数化后训练更新的子空间选择能有效控制生成长度，在不损害偏好对齐质量的前提下降低推理成本。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [124] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对语音大语言模型（Speech-LLMs）中声学编码器与语言解码器更新范数差异导致单池差分隐私裁剪失效的问题，提出α-split双池分配方法，在保持DP保证不变的前提下恢复模型效用并增强编码器抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐层差分隐私（DP）裁剪方法在联邦学习中通过按参数量比例分配裁剪预算来提高梯度保真度，但在Speech-LLMs中，由于声学编码器和语言解码器的更新范数存在数量级差异，导致“跨组件预算崩溃”，严重损害词错误率（WER）或导致训练失败。

Method: 提出了$\alpha$-split方法，这是一种基于双池的预算分配策略，将编码器和LLM参数归入独立的池中。该方法对编码器LLM参数进行归一化处理，并证明了联合$\ell_2$敏感性和原始$(\varepsilon, \delta)$-DP保证保持不变。

Result: 1. 诊断了六种逐层方法和三种Speech-LLM架构中的根本原因，证实崩溃严重程度与组件间范数比率成正比。
2. 在架构校准的$\alpha$值下，该方法恢复了与平坦DP相当的WER效用。
3. 相比仅增加2.6%的LLM噪声开销，为编码器提供了4.47倍更紧致的每组件噪声保护，有效抵御基于说话人声音的梯度反转攻击。

Conclusion: $\alpha$-split双池分配策略解决了Speech-LLMs中因组件范数不平衡导致的差分隐私预算崩溃问题，在维持隐私保障的同时显著提升了模型性能和安全性。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [125] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究指出，尽管大型语言模型（LLM）能识别新闻框架，但难以在保持事实不变的前提下逆转已知的框架变换。通过控制反转测试发现，事实保留率高（约0.84），但框架逆转率极低（0.044-0.068），表明“识别框架”不等于“能够消除框架”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的框架研究主要评估生成质量、检测能力或文本中性化程度，缺乏直接验证模型能否在固定事实的基础上撤销已知框架变换的能力。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究LLM在框架逆转方面的真实能力。

Method: 构建了一个受控的反转测试实验，涵盖三种既定的文本框架实现方式：评价性词汇（evaluative lexis）、能动性实现（agency realization）和信息显著性（information salience）。实验使用60篇新闻文章和三种干预强度，生成了540对具有原子事实 preserved 且记录编辑的配对变体。在Qwen、DeepSeek和Kimi三个大语言模型上进行了评估。

Result: 在所有模型中，事实保留率保持在较高水平（约0.84），但干预逆转率极低（介于0.044至0.068之间）。即使在正确识别框架类型和方向的情况下，综合逆转率也仅为0.071。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了事实保真度、框架识别与框架逆转之间存在明显的分离现象。确认一篇文章的框架并不隐含模型能够将其逆转，表明当前LLM在消除特定叙事框架方面存在显著局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [126] [The widening evaluation gap in medical large language model research 2023 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11770)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 医学研究评估大语言模型的速度严重滞后于模型迭代速度，导致大多数临床试验在发布时已评估过时或停用的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究医学研究是否跟上了快速迭代的临床系统（大语言模型）的评估需求，分析“评估滞后”现象及其对研究有效性的影响。

Method: 分析PubMed数据库中2023年1月至2026年6月间14个临床领域的11,628条记录；计算从最新模型发布到论文发表的时间间隔（评估滞后）；通过反事实基准测试（固定模型组成）衡量模型老化漂移与迁移至新系统的抵消效果；比较不同研究设计（如随机对照试验 vs 其他设计）在模型选择上的差异。

Result: 文献数量增长45倍，但仅2.5%采用随机对照或前瞻性设计；评估滞后从1.33季度扩大至6.08季度；迁移到新系统仅能抵消56%的模型老化漂移；随机对照试验评估的模型平均比非随机设计老4.6个季度；62%的随机对照试验评估的是已停用模型家族；对于仍在开发中的模型，不同研究设计无显著差异。

Conclusion: 严谨性与时效性存在张力，这种张力主要源于模型选择策略而非研究时间线本身；当前医学研究在评估LLM时面临严重的时效性问题，多数证据基于过时模型。

Abstract: Large language models are superseded every few quarters; clinical evidence takes years. We asked whether medical research is keeping pace with the systems it evaluates. PubMed returned 11,628 records for January 2023 to June 2026 across fourteen clinical domains, growing 45-fold; 2.5% used a randomised, controlled or prospective design. Evaluation lag, from a study's newest named model release to its own publication, widened from 1.33 to 6.08 quarters. Because discontinued models age mechanically, we benchmarked this against a counterfactual holding model composition fixed: migration to newer systems offset only 56% of the drift (95% CI 50-65). Randomised trials evaluated models a median 4.6 quarters older than other designs (P = 3 x 10^-19), yet among studies naming a model still under development no design differed from any other; 62% of randomised trials evaluated a discontinued family. Rigour and currency are in tension, and that tension reflects model selection rather than research timelines.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对低资源、重声调语言（约鲁巴语）与英语的代码切换语音，评估了11个现代ASR和音频LM系统。发现传统WER指标掩盖了代码切换行为：最佳ASR在局部诊断指标上显著劣于领先的音频LM，且约鲁巴语识别率极低，错误集中在切换至约鲁巴语的节点。研究还揭示了生成式音频LM存在的翻译、冗余及提示泄露问题，并开源了相关评估工具以支持可复现的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动语音识别（ASR）系统在单语基准上表现优异，但其在低资源、重声调语言（如约鲁巴语）的代码切换场景下的行为尚不明确。现有评估方法难以捕捉代码切换特有的误差模式，亟需更细致的评估框架来揭示模型在处理混合语言时的真实性能瓶颈。

Method: 构建了包含2000条 utterance 的确定性评估集，使用统一的评分管道对6个ASR模型和5个音频LM进行评估。除了传统的词错率（WER），引入了开关本地化诊断指标，包括开关入口令牌错误率（SETER）、窗口化开关点错误率、特定语言错误率以及忽略声调的WER，以精细分析系统在代码切换处的表现。

Result: 1. 聚合WER掩盖了代码切换行为：最佳ASR系统在统计上与领先音频LM的WER无显著差异，但在所有开关本地化指标上，音频LM显著优于ASR。
2. 语言不平衡：约鲁巴语令牌识别几乎崩溃（错误率高达0.97），而英语识别较好；错误主要集中在切换进入约鲁巴语时。
3. 生成式缺陷：部分生成式音频LM无法作为精确转录器，存在依赖提示的翻译、过度冗长及提示泄露现象。
4. 开源贡献：发布了清单、度量实现和评估脚本，支持非洲代码切换语音的可复现评估。

Conclusion: 传统WER指标不足以全面评估代码切换语音识别系统的性能，必须结合开关本地化诊断指标。音频LM在处理代码切换的局部精度上优于传统ASR，但需注意其生成特性带来的幻觉问题。该研究为低资源多语言环境下的语音识别评估提供了新的基准和方法论支持。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [128] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现心血管筛查模型的高准确率（AUROC ~0.89）主要源于特征泄漏而非模型能力。移除诊断后特征后，所有模型性能显著下降并趋于一致。可解释增强机器（EBM）在保持高性能的同时速度更快，且通过调整提高了公平性。结论指出：评估实践和特征选择是性能瓶颈，而非模型容量；透明度和校准对确保公平性和可靠性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证国家健康调查中心血管筛查模型的高AUROC是否反映真实学习能力还是目标泄漏；评估表格基础模型的表现；考察部署所需的公平性、校准和可解释性等属性。

Method: 在2022年BRFSS数据（442,067人）上基准测试10种分类器（线性、树集成、神经、玻璃盒、表格基础模型），按5个特征层级（降低泄漏风险）进行评估。审计指标包括歧视度、校准、公平性、共形覆盖、解释忠实度和推理成本。使用2023年数据（430,755人）进行冻结模型迁移测试。

Result: 移除两个诊断后特征导致所有模型AUROC下降0.049-0.051，性能差异缩小至0.0045的窄带。可解释增强机器（EBM）在非劣效性范围内优于其他模型，且比最强基础模型快约104倍。初始阈值下女性心肌梗塞检出率（75.4%）低于男性（89.0%），但通过编辑形状函数将差距缩小至0.010。共形预测在不同群体中覆盖率不一，Mondrian校准修复了各层级的校准问题。冻结模型迁移时AUROC变化小于0.002。

Conclusion: 文献中报告的模型性能优势主要是特征集的属性，而非学习算法本身。透明度未带来显著成本，但使公平性修复和不确定性条件化变得可直接审计。评估实践（如特征选择和校准）而非模型容量是当前的主要约束条件。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [129] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体中印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语混合文本的Token级语言识别问题，通过微调MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa模型，并构建基准数据集及混合生成方法，实现了有效的多语言上下文嵌入识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单语言模型不适用于代码混合（code-mixed）文本中的Token级语言识别；用户在单次表达中频繁切换多种语言，导致准确识别混合Token的语言标签成为迫切需求。

Method: 将任务建模为序列标注问题，针对印度语言微调基于Transformer的上下文模型（MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa）；提出两种基于三种语言平行句子的代码混合生成方法；在印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语三种数据配置上进行评估。

Result: 验证了上下文嵌入在多元社交媒体文本Token级语言识别中的有效性；发布了包含人工标注测试集的代码混合Token语言识别基准数据集，并公开了微调后的模型以支持可重复性研究。

Conclusion: 微调的多语言Transformer模型能有效解决代码混合场景下的细粒度语言识别问题，所发布的基准和数据集为后续研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [130] [Epistemic orientation predicts legislative effectiveness among members of the US Congress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11865)
*Segun Aroyehun,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过证据-直觉（EMI）分数分析发现，议员在国会演讲和推文中使用的证据导向语言程度存在个体差异。意识形态极端的议员使用较少的证据导向语言；同一议员在不同平台上的证据导向倾向具有一致性；且证据导向语言与立法有效性呈正相关，表明证据导向不仅是整体话语特征，更是衡量个人立法效能的重要指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究表明美国国会议事厅演讲中证据导向语言自1970年代中期以来呈下降趋势，但现有研究多聚焦于议会层面。本研究旨在将分析单位从众议院转向个体议员，探究认知取向是否在议员间存在系统性差异，以及这种差异是否与政治行为和立法有效性相关。

Method: 使用“证据-直觉”（EMI）分数测量议员在国会 floor speeches 和 Twitter 帖子中证据导向与直觉导向语言的相对 prevalence。将这些指标与议员的意识形态、制度地位、沟通语境及立法有效性得分（LES）等数据相关联，并控制多种变量进行分析。

Result: 1. 意识形态越极端的议员，在国会演讲中使用证据导向语言越少。2. EMI具有跨平台一致性：在floor speech中更多使用证据导向语言的议员，在Twitter上也更倾向于使用证据导向语言，尽管Twitter整体的EMI分数较低。3. 在控制意识形态及其他众多变量后，国会演讲中的EMI分数与个体立法有效性呈正相关。

Conclusion: 证据导向语言不仅是国会话语的聚合特征，也是个体层面立法沟通和有效性的有意义属性。强调基于证据和事实的沟通对于民主治理、问责制和集体决策的重要性，并指出提升立法有效性可能与增强证据导向的语言使用有关。

Abstract: Truth and evidence-based communication provide important foundations for democratic governance, accountability, and collective decision-making. Prior work shows that evidence-oriented language in US congressional floor speeches has declined since the mid-1970s, alongside broader changes in legislative productivity and polarization. This study shifts the analysis from congressional sessions to individual members of Congress to examine whether epistemic orientation varies systematically across legislators and whether it relates to political behavior and legislative effectiveness. Using the Evidence-Minus-Intuition (EMI) score, we measure the relative prevalence of evidence-oriented versus intuition-oriented language in congressional floor speeches and Twitter posts. We link these measures to legislator-level data on ideology, institutional position, communication context, and Legislative Effectiveness Score (LES). The results show that more ideologically extreme members use less evidence-oriented language on the congressional floor. EMI also exhibits cross-platform consistency with members who use more evidence-oriented language in floor speeches also being more evidence-oriented on Twitter, although EMI is lower on Twitter overall. Finally, EMI in congressional speeches is positively associated with individual legislative effectiveness, even after accounting for ideology and extensive political, institutional, demographic, topical, and communication volume controls. These findings suggest that evidence-oriented language is not only an aggregate feature of congressional discourse but also a meaningful attribute of individual-level legislative communication and effectiveness.

</details>


### [131] [Augustinian BabyLM: What Ostensive Definition Can and Cannot Teach a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11870)
*Lisa Bylinina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了视觉初始化对小型掩码语言模型（DeBERTa）的影响。结果显示，视觉种子在特定词汇上产生持久优势，尤其在对象-属性知识中表现明显，但在常规语法基准测试中效果不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证奥古斯丁的“指示性”词学习理论，即通过视觉 grounding 赋予单词初始含义，探究这种视觉初始化是否能在语言模型的训练过程中留下可测量的印记，并影响其最终的语言能力。

Method: 使用10M词的小型语料库训练DeBERTa模型。在训练前，为与图像区域对应的标记提供基于视觉的嵌入（视觉种子），其余标记随机初始化。构建了定制的Visual-Property Swap基准测试，并在因果测试中通过合成grounding未种子化单词来验证优势转移。

Result: 1. 视觉初始化在训练中留下持久印记，但大多数BabyLM基准测试无法检测到这一影响。2. 在零样本对象-属性知识（COMPS）任务中，视觉种子在所有配置下均带来性能提升。3. 视觉种子带来的优势局限于被种子化的单词，且具有复现性。4. 虚词和抽象词汇即使获得强视觉种子也能保留该优势并降低掩码预测损失，尽管现有基准未能捕捉到这一现象。

Conclusion: 视觉初始化确实能增强模型对具体对象属性的理解，但这种优势主要局限于被种子化的词汇且难以被现有的抽象语法基准所检测。目前尚不清楚何种评估方法能有效捕捉虚词和抽象词汇中由视觉种子带来的潜在改进，这是一个待解决的开放性问题。

Abstract: A language model normally begins training with random word embeddings: whatever 'banana' means must be learned from training corpora. I implement St. Augustine's picture of word learning, meaning by ostension, for a small masked language model (DeBERTa) trained on 10M words: before training, visually grounded tokens receive embeddings derived from the image regions they label; other tokens start random. Visual initialization leaves a measurable imprint that lasts until the end of training. At the same time, the effect remains invisible under most BabyLM benchmarks, which probe abstract grammatical knowledge: visual initialization does not affect performance there. The only zero-shot exception is object-property knowledge (COMPS, Misra et al. 2023), where seeding helps in every configuration. To follow up on this result, I build a corpus-tailored version of the Visual-Property Swap benchmark (Lin et al., 2026), which tests color, material, size, and shape knowledge, with per-item training frequency and seeded status. Here, vision-seeded models have a persistent, seed- replicated advantage, confined to the seeded words. As a causal test, I show that synthetic grounding of previously unseeded words transfers the advantage to exactly those words. Function words and abstract vocabulary also receive strong visual seeds and retain them throughout training, and the training objective draws on them: held-out mask-prediction loss falls for these words in every seed. However, no benchmark I run registers this. What evaluation would pick this up remains an open question.

</details>


### [132] [Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11878)
*Varun Teja Chundru,Debasmita Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合DeBERTa-v3、MC Dropout和温度缩放校准的多信号幻觉检测流水线，在HaluEval上达到F1=0.915。利用DPO优化Qwen2.5-0.5B模型将幻觉率降低55.9%。研究发现领域匹配预训练（如PubMedBERT在SciFact上）比通用域迁移更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型生成的文本中存在的不忠实主张（即“幻觉”问题），旨在通过多信号检测流水线提高幻觉检测的准确性，并探索减少生成模型幻觉的有效策略。

Method: 1. 构建多信号检测流水线：结合微调的DeBERTa-v3分类器、蒙特卡洛(Dropout)不确定性量化和温度缩放校准。
2. 使用直接偏好优化(DPO)对Qwen2.5-0.5B生成器进行后处理优化。
3. 进行上下文消融实验以验证模型的推理能力。
4. 评估不同领域预训练模型（通用域 vs. 生物医学域PubMedBERT）在特定基准上的表现。

Result: 1. 检测性能：在HaluEval通用任务中F1=0.915，AUROC=0.977；QA、Summarization、Dialogue任务F1分别为0.97、0.96、0.82。加入MC Dropout后准确率提升至93.2%。
2. 推理验证：移除知识上下文导致摘要任务F1下降24%，证实模型进行真实的蕴含推理而非表面模式匹配。
3. 数据效率：25%的训练数据即可达到全量数据77%的性能。
4. 生成优化：应用DPO后，Qwen2.5-0.5B的幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%（相对减少55.9%）。
5. 领域适应性：通用域训练在SciFact生物医学基准上表现较差(F1=0.52)，而领域匹配的PubMedBERT微调后达到F1=0.63，AUROC=0.81。

Conclusion: 提出的多信号检测流水线能高效准确检测LLM幻觉。DPO可有效降低生成模型的幻觉率。研究表明，领域特定的预训练和微调是实现跨领域鲁棒性最强的适应策略，优于简单的通用域迁移。

Abstract: Large language models generate fluent text that can contain unfaithful claims -- a phenomenon known as hallucination. We present a multi-signal detection pipeline combining fine-tuned DeBERTa-v3 classification, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty quantification, and temperature-scaled calibration for response-level hallucination detection. Evaluated on the HaluEval benchmark, our pipeline achieves F1=0.915 and AUROC=0.977 on general-domain tasks, with per-task F1 scores of 0.97 (QA), 0.96 (Summarization), and 0.82 (Dialogue). MC Dropout inference further improves accuracy to 93.2%. A context ablation study confirms the model performs genuine entailment reasoning rather than exploiting surface patterns, with summarization F1 dropping 24% when knowledge context is removed. Learning curve analysis reveals that 25% of training data captures 77% of full-data performance. Beyond detection, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to a Qwen2.5-0.5B generator, reducing its hallucination rate from 85.5% to 37.7% (55.9% relative reduction) as measured by our detector. Cross-domain evaluation on the SciFact biomedical benchmark shows that general-domain training transfers poorly (F1=0.52), motivating domain-specific fine-tuning. PubMedBERT fine-tuned on SciFact achieves F1=0.63 and AUROC=0.81, demonstrating that domain-matched pre-training is the strongest adaptation strategy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/varunteja99/hallucination-detection-nlp

</details>


### [133] [Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11892)
*Yingzhi Wang,Reem Alhazzani,Muhammad Alqurishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Nuha-Speech倡议，通过构建大规模阿拉伯语语音问答数据集、微调Qwen-Omni模型及设计评估框架，解决阿拉伯语在语音大模型中代表性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多语言语音大模型的发展，阿拉伯语仍严重缺乏代表性，亟需专门的基础设施来训练和评估阿拉伯语语音大模型。

Method: 1. 构建包含超过150万个训练样本的大规模阿拉伯语语音问答（SQA）语料库；2. 基于不同规模的Qwen-Omni模型变体进行监督微调；3. 设计包含多样化任务和定制指标的评估框架。

Result: 建立了涵盖数据集构建、模型训练和系统评估的完整基础设施，实现了针对广泛核心语音任务的指令微调。

Conclusion: 在阿拉伯语语音资源有限的约束下，为阿拉伯语语音大模型奠定了基础性基础设施。

Abstract: As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

</details>


### [134] [Distance generalization in transformers: why bother with positional encoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11913)
*Daniel Henrik Nevermann,Claudius Gros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究聚焦于Transformer模型的“距离泛化”能力（即训练与推理期间token间距离变化时的性能），通过构建合成延迟复制任务，系统评估了位置编码方案、数据多样性及迁移学习效果对模型处理未见距离的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管针对Transformer的“分布外长度泛化”已有大量研究，但关于“距离泛化”（在固定上下文长度下，改变token间相对距离时的性能表现）的研究尚不充分，亟需深入理解其底层机制。

Method: 构建了两种涉及有限源-召回距离的合成延迟复制任务（完全复制和选择性复制），并在训练阶段未见的延迟距离上测试模型性能，以探究三个核心问题：(A) RoPE和ALiBi等位置编码是否优于无位置编码；(B) 训练数据中看到的token间距离多样性如何影响性能；(C) 距离迁移学习何时产生正或负效果。

Result: 研究发现，提升对底层机制的理解至关重要。具体而言，不同位置编码方案（如RoPE、ALiBi vs NoPE）在距离分辨力上存在差异，且训练数据的多样性显著影响模型性能，距离迁移学习的效果具有条件依赖性（可能为正或负）。

Conclusion: 强调了对Transformer距离泛化机制进行深入理解的必要性，为优化模型在处理可变token间距任务中的表现提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution length generalization, namely to extrapolate a task from short to longer context, has been studied intensively for transformers. Here we focus on distance generalization, which probes performance when inter-token distances are changed between training and inference, while keeping a fixed context length. We construct two synthetic delay copy tasks, both involving finite distances between source and recall, where tokens are copied either fully or selectively, and test models on delays unseen during training. We address three questions: (A) Do positional encoding schemes such as RoPE and ALiBi improve distance resolution relative to no positional encoding (NoPE)? (B) How does data diversity, the number of inter-token distances seen in training, affect performance? (C) When is distance transfer learning positive or negative? We present a thorough investigation, finding that it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [OmniTable: A Unified Wide-Table System for Petabyte-Scale LLM Data Curation and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11148)
*Yuzhuo Fu,Xiangchun Wang,Chao Huang,Liyi Wang,Binwei Zeng,Yuhan Wang,Taotao Nie,Dongke Hu,Wang Hong,Jiayi Wang,Wenwen Cui,Zhuyan Zhou,Yushun Guo,Yuhan Xing,Jiaxin Lian,Peng Lin,Qing Cui,Wenhui Shi,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OmniTable 是一种针对 PB 级 LLM 数据策展的统一宽表架构，通过逻辑统一与物理分离解决数据分散、人工编排和低效问题，将人工策展周期从 14 天缩短至 2.5 天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级大模型开发面临 PB 级非结构化数据分散在数百张物理表中、特征工程依赖手动管道编排以及数据血缘缺失等瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于“逻辑统一、物理分离”的 OmniTable 架构：(1) 统一宽表抽象整合异构数据；(2) 声明式特征生命周期管理实现自动化依赖解析与血缘追踪；(3) 自适应执行引擎支持异构计算路由（CPU/GPU）及容错；(4) 混合加速数据探索结合全局 ID索引和 OLAP卸载实现秒级查询。

Result: 在生产环境中管理超过 35 PB 的训练数据（涵盖网页、代码、PDF 和 SFT 领域），将人工介入的数据策展周期从约 14 天减少至约 2.5 天（提升 5.6 倍），并实现了稳定的特征版本控制和可审计的血缘关系。

Conclusion: OmniTable 成功解决了大规模 LLM 数据策展的效率与治理难题，显著降低了人工成本并提升了数据处理吞吐量与稳定性。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical bottleneck in industrial-grade LLM development, where petabyte-scale unstructured corpora are scattered across hundreds of physical tables, feature engineering relies on manual, table-centric pipeline orchestration, and data lineage is largely absent. We present OmniTable as an architecture blueprint for a unified wide-table layer built on Logical Unification, Physical Separation, targeting petabyte-scale LLM data curation and exploration. OmniTable makes four contributions: (1) a unified wide-table abstraction that consolidates multi-source heterogeneous data and thousands of derived features under a single logical schema via logical-physical mapping; (2) declarative feature lifecycle management that automates dependency resolution, execution planning, operator fusion, and lineage tracking, replacing manual pipeline orchestration with a "declare-and-execute" paradigm; (3) an adaptive execution engine with autonomous governance that achieves stable PB-scale feature backfill through heterogeneous compute routing (CPU/GPU), adaptive tuning, UDF-level fault tolerance, and automated storage layout optimization; and (4) hybrid-accelerated data exploration combining a global ID index, transparent OLAP offloading, and background materialized views to deliver second-level point lookups and filtered exports exceeding 20 TB/hour. In production, OmniTable manages over 35 PB of training data across web, code, PDF, and SFT domains, reducing the human-in-the-loop curation cycle from approximately 14 days to approximately 2.5 days (5.6x over the pre-OmniTable production workflow), with consistent feature versioning, auditable lineage, and minimal manual intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [When Synthetic Data Hurts: On Catastrophic Forgetting in Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10750)
*Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Utkarsh Soni,Eugene Wen,Arvid Frydenlund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体从大规模技能库中检索技能的挑战，本研究提出了一种包含34,396个技能的技能路由器及大规模检索研究。研究发现合成数据微调虽提升分布内检索，但导致对真实和分布外（OOD）数据的灾难性遗忘。通过引入持续学习中的遗忘缓解方法（如嵌入锚点正则化、LwF、EWC等），不仅保留了OOD性能，还将0.6B Qwen检索器的合成数据检索准确率提升了13.98%，为稀缺多正样本监督提供了鲁棒的微调方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地依赖运行时检索的外部技能，如何从庞大的技能库中准确选择技能成为关键挑战。现有的检索方法在利用有限真实监督和合成数据进行微调时，面临分布内性能提升与分布外/真实数据性能下降（灾难性遗忘）之间的矛盾。

Method: 构建了一个包含34,396个技能的规模化技能路由器；使用有限真实监督和合成数据进行技能检索的微调训练；评估并应用了受持续学习启发的多种遗忘缓解微调策略，包括嵌入锚点正则化（Embedding-Anchor Regularization）、无遗忘学习（Learning without Forgetting, LwF）、弹性权重巩固（Elastic Weight Consolidation, EWC）以及L2初始化方法。

Result: 1. 合成数据微调虽然提高了分布内检索效果，但在真实数据和分布外（OOD）数据上导致了灾难性遗忘。2. 应用的遗忘缓解方法成功保持了OOD技能检索的性能。3. 对于0.6B参数的Qwen检索器和重排器，这些方法使合成分布内技能的检索性能提升了13.98%。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个实用的基准测试和一个鲁棒的微调配方，特别适用于稀缺且多正样本的监督场景，证明了结合持续学习技术的微调策略能有效解决大规模技能检索中的泛化与遗忘问题。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external skills retrieved at runtime, making skill selection from large repositories a critical challenge. We present a production skill router over 34,396 skills and a large-scale study of skill retrieval using limited real supervision and synthetic data. We found that the synthetic-data fine-tuning improves in-distribution retrieval but it causes catastrophic forgetting on real and out-of-distribution (OOD) data. We evaluate several forgetting mitigation fine-tuning approaches inspired by continual learning, including embedding-anchor regularization, Learning without Forgetting (LwF), Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), and L2-initialization. The results show that these approaches not only retain the performance on OOD skills retrieval but also improve the retrieval on synthetic in-distribution skills by 13.98\% for 0.6B Qwen retriever and reranker. Our results provide a practical benchmark and a robust fine-tuning recipe for scarce, multi-positive supervision.

</details>


### [137] [UniRec: Cross-stage Multi-Task Fusion with Preference Alignment for Cascaded Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11052)
*Lingyuan Kong,Jiaqi Cui,Fanjiao Zeng,Congqi Wang,Yu Li,Yuan Cheng,Jingxin Liu,Xiaoshuang Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出UniRec模型，通过共享嵌入、双轴偏好对齐及属性组相对正则化，实现推荐系统中预排序与排序阶段的联合优化，解决跨阶段不一致性问题，在快手平台上线并提升0.616%用户时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常采用级联架构（如预排序和排序），独立优化各阶段会导致跨阶段不一致性：上游模型可能过滤掉下游偏好的物品，且下游的独立融合会抵消上游改进。现有方法多聚焦于单阶段内的多目标融合，或仅在跨阶段中添加简单的下游分数因子，缺乏对跨阶段融合模块的联合优化探索。

Method: 提出UniRec统一跨阶段推荐融合模型。1）两个融合代理部分共享输入嵌入，并在单一计算图中训练，使梯度能通过共享表示反向传播并相互影响；2）引入双轴偏好对齐目标：垂直方向通过一致性项将下游成对偏好传递至上游融合分数，水平方向通过紧凑聚合项将异构先验信号中的多个成对目标重组为双向偏好证据；3）添加属性组相对正则化，通过在属性组内计算优势并对策略进行归一化，防止优化过程过度集中在高奖励区域而忽视其他目标。

Result: 离线实验表明，UniRec consistently 优于单阶段融合及跨阶段协调基线方法。在线A/B测试显示，应用使用时长提升了0.616%。该模型已在快手平台全面部署。

Conclusion: UniRec通过端到端的联合优化机制有效解决了推荐系统级联架构中的跨阶段不一致问题，实现了性能提升并成功应用于大规模工业生产环境。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems use cascaded stages with different objectives, feature spaces, and latency constraints. Optimizing pre-ranking and ranking separately can create cross-stage inconsistency: upstream models may filter out items preferred by downstream rankers, and independently tuned downstream fusion can offset upstream improvements. Existing multi-task fusion methods focus on multi-objective fusion within the ranking stage, and cross-stage methods typically only add a downstream score factor to upstream ranking. Joint optimization of fusion modules across both stages remains largely unexplored.
  We propose UniRec, a Unified Cross-stage Recommendation Fusion model. First, the two fusion agents partially share input embeddings and are trained in a single computation graph, so gradients from either stage propagate through the shared representation and influence the other. Second, we introduce a dual-axis preference alignment objective: a vertical cross-stage consistency term transfers downstream pairwise preferences to the upstream fusion score, and a horizontal compact aggregation term reorganizes dozens of pairwise objectives over heterogeneous prior signals into bidirectional preference evidence. Third, we find that unconstrained end-to-end fusion optimization can exploit imbalances in item attribute distributions, over-concentrating on high-reward regions at the cost of other objectives. We therefore add an attribute group-relative regularization that computes advantages within attribute groups and normalizes the policy over the same groups, so uniformly promoting an entire high-reward group yields no optimization gain. Offline, UniRec consistently outperforms single-stage fusion and cross-stage coordination baselines. Online A/B tests show a 0.616\% gain in app usage duration. UniRec is fully deployed on the Kuaishou platform.

</details>


### [138] [VikingRAG: Accurate and Token-efficient Retrieval-augmented Generation over Structured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11390)
*Peiyuan Gao,Gaoyuan Zhang,Haojie Qin,Yahui Sun,Qianyi Zhang,Yunhao Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 VikingRAG，一种通过目录感知语义管理和检索轨迹复用，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低 RAG 系统 Token 消耗的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的检索增强生成（RAG）方法虽利用文档结构获取证据，但往往产生高昂的 Token 成本。旨在减少结构上下文 Token 开销的同时不损害高精度。

Method: 1. 构建 VikingRAG 系统，集成语义与结构访问以支持高效的多轮检索；2. 将多轮检索轨迹物化为“经验边”并复用，避免重复探索；3. 引入自适应升级策略，仅在证据不足时触发多轮检索，否则使用单轮检索回答。

Result: 基础系统 VikingRAG 在匹配 SOTA 精度的同时，仅消耗其 11.6%-51.9% 的 Token；结合轨迹复用和自适应升级后，Token 成本进一步降至 5.1%-32.5%，且保持具有竞争力的准确性和存储性能。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了 RAG 系统的 Token 开销，证明了其在新兴 AI 知识库中的实用价值。

Abstract: State-of-the-art retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods exploit document structures to acquire sufficient evidence, but often incur substantial token costs. To reduce structural-context tokens without compromising high RAG accuracy, we present {\sf VikingRAG}, a directory-aware semantic data management system that tightly integrates semantic and structural access to support structural-context-efficient, evidence-gap-driven multi-round retrieval. To further reduce token overhead of multi-round interaction, we materialize agentic multi-round retrieval traces as experience edges, and reuse these edges for similar queries, avoiding repeated multi-round exploration. To additionally reduce token costs when agentic multi-round retrieval is unnecessary, we introduce an adaptive escalation strategy that answers from one-round experience-augmented retrieval when the evidence is sufficient, and invokes agentic multi-round retrieval only otherwise. Experiments on real datasets show that the base system {\sf VikingRAG} matches high accuracy of state-of-the-art methods while consuming only 11.6\%--51.9\% of their tokens. With retrieval-trace reuse and adaptive escalation, token costs drop to 5.1\%--32.5\% while maintaining competitive accuracy and practical document-storage performance, showing the utility of this work for emerging AI knowledge bases.

</details>


### [139] [TimelyRAG: Semantic-Temporal Hybrid Retrieval for Time-Critical Question Answering in Overlapping-Evolving Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11572)
*Youngeun Nam,Joeun Kim,Hwanjun Song,Susik Yoon,Jae-Gil Lee,Byung Suk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TimelyRAG框架和TimelyQABench基准，通过引入时间距离解决重叠演进环境（如法律政策修订）中的检索增强生成可靠性问题，显著提升nDCG@10指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和RAG在处理文档演变时不可靠；现有方法仅适用于离散演进环境，无法处理法律、政策等法规中常见的“重叠演进”场景（即修订版覆盖旧条款但保留大部分内容，导致版本间语义高度重叠）。

Method: 提出TimelyRAG：一个与检索器无关的框架，将时间距离纳入排序过程以对齐查询与版本适配的文档；构建TimelyQABench：首个针对重法规领域及重叠演进挑战的基准测试。

Result: 实验显示性能持续提升，nDCG@10最高提升28.6%，验证了时序推理在演进文档QA中的重要性。

Conclusion: 引入时间维度可有效提升重叠演进环境下文档检索的准确性，为法规类领域的可靠QA提供了新方案。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) have advanced open-domain question answering (QA), they remain unreliable when documents evolve through amendments. Existing time-sensitive retrieval methods address only the disjoint-evolving environment, where each update is an independent snapshot. However, laws, policies, and regulations often operate in overlapping-evolving environments, where amendments override earlier clauses while preserving most content, creating strong semantic overlap across versions. We propose TimelyRAG, a retriever-agnostic framework that incorporates temporal distance into ranking to align queries with version-appropriate documents. We also introduce TimelyQABench, the first benchmark for regulation-heavy domains with overlapping-evolving challenges. Experiments show consistent gains, up to +28.6% in nDCG@10, highlighting the importance of temporal reasoning for reliable QA over evolving documents. All resources are available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/TimelyRAG.

</details>


### [140] [FedHUR: Learning Hierarchical Utility-Guided Client Relations for Personalized Federated Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11632)
*Mingzhe Han,Jiahao Liu,Dongsheng Li,Jiankui Zhou,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FedHUR框架，通过层级效用引导客户端关系学习，解决联邦推荐中全局关系无法捕捉用户层级和多粒度特性的问题，显著提升个性化推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐方法的聚合权重基于预定义的参数相似性或互补性假设，仅构建单一全局关系，无法反映用户关系的层级性和多粒度特性，且不能直接体现聚合后对预测性能的改善效果。

Method: 提出FedHUR框架，以item-item过滤器为对象构建关系和聚合。首先聚合聚类各客户端本地信息获取全局层级信息；每个客户端根据本地信息和全局层级信息计算层级效用信号，指示哪些协作信息有助于提升其预测；服务器利用这些效用信号检索对该客户端有用的其他客户端进行个性化聚合。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，FedHUR一致优于现有的联邦推荐基线方法，证明了层级效用引导的客户端关系学习的有效性。

Conclusion: FedHUR通过引入层级效用信号指导客户端间的个性化聚合，有效解决了传统方法在关系建模上的局限性，提升了联邦推荐系统的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Federated recommendation enables collaborative model training while keeping user interaction data on local clients. A central problem in federated recommendation is how to aggregate useful information across clients for personalized recommendation. Existing personalized aggregation methods usually construct client relations from predefined parameter-based assumptions, such as parameter similarity or complementarity, and use these relations to determine aggregation weights. However, such methods construct a single global relation, which is insufficient to capture the hierarchical and multi-granularity nature of user relations in recommendation. Moreover, these predefined relations cannot directly reflect whether the related clients can improve prediction performance after aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose FedHUR, a federated recommendation framework for learning hierarchical utility-guided client relations. FedHUR takes item-item filters as the object for relation construction and aggregation. Specifically, it first aggregates and clusters each client's local information to obtain global hierarchical information. Each client computes hierarchical utility signals based on its local information and the global hierarchical information, indicating which collaborative information is useful for improving its prediction. The server uses these utility signals to retrieve clients that are useful to that client for further personalized aggregation. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that FedHUR consistently outperforms existing federated recommendation baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical utility-guided client relation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/Mingzhe-Han/FedHUR.

</details>


### [141] [Your Retriever Already Knows: Distribution-Shape QPP for RAG Retrieval Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11646)
*Matyáš Veselý,Michal Průšek,Jiří Franc*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究比较了三种检索增强生成（RAG）中的查询性能预测（QPP）范式，发现仅使用24个非词汇特征的GeneralQPP在速度和成本上优于LLM法官，而混合方法在对抗性检测等特定场景下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG管道缺乏可靠的推理时信号来判断检索是否成功，导致在模糊或超出范围查询时容易幻觉。特别是在数据敏感且禁止使用第三方LLM API的场景（如捷克核监管部署）中，需要高效的本地化评估方案。

Method: 在ViDoRe视觉域（14,514个查询）和SÚJB数据集（含1,510个查询，包括500个合成对抗样本）上，对比了三种QPP范式：基于分数的特征（GeneralQPP）、基于内容的LLM法官（本地Qwen3.5）以及两者的混合模式。

Result: GeneralQPP（2ms/查询）达到0.856的加权平均AUROC，显著优于经典QPP文献池（0.835）和本地LLM法官（0.649），且速度快约3000倍、成本低。混合方法在ViDoRe上表现相当（0.863），但在SÚJB数据集上通过统计显著优势达到0.911 AUROC和0.954对抗检测率。跨数据集排名一致性高（Spearman ρ= 0.90）。留一域交叉验证显示，精简版GeneralQPP（S1-Lean）能恢复大部分性能。

Conclusion: 非词汇特征提取的高效QPP方法在大多数情况下提供了速度与性能的平衡，而在需要高鲁棒性或对抗性检测的场景中，结合LLM判断的混合策略能提供更优结果。该方法为数据敏感环境下的RAG系统提供了可行的本地化评估方案。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines often provide no reliable inference-time signal of whether retrieval succeeded; on ambiguous or out-of-scope queries, generation may then hallucinate. Motivated by a Czech nuclear-regulator deployment where data sensitivity precludes third-party LLM APIs, we compare three Query Performance Prediction (QPP) paradigms for retrieval sufficiency in RAG: score-based features, a content-based LLM judge, and a hybrid. On the eight ViDoRe vision domains (14,514 queries), our 24 non-lexical features (GeneralQPP; 15 distribution-shape, 5 query-surface, 4 global) reach a weighted-average AUROC of 0.856 at 2 ms per query, ahead of a classic-QPP literature pool (Classic Full, 0.835) and well above a local Qwen3.5 LLM judge (0.649, +0.207 gap; $\sim$3000$\times$ faster and cheaper per query). Adding the LLM judgment as one feature (hybrid) matches S1 on ViDoRe (0.863) but gains a statistically significant edge on SÚJB (AUROC 0.911 at Hit@5, adversarial-detection 0.954; 1,510 queries, 500 synthetic adversarial), at LLM latency. Rankings agree across datasets (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Under Leave-One-Domain-Out, S1 drops to 0.706; a 13-feature LODO-stepwise subset (S1-Lean) recovers to 0.719 (+0.032 over the literature pool).

</details>


### [142] [Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings For Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11808)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Talal Aloushan,Rose Khairoalsendi,Jana Shata,Mohammed Alhassan,Leen Alrehaili,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GLIE（生成式晚期交互嵌入），利用向量在单位球面上且集中在低维流形上的几何特性，通过极少数的生成向量重建完整嵌入集，实现存储高效且高精度的视觉文档检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的晚期交互检索方法虽然准确但存储开销大；现有压缩方法在严格存储预算下性能急剧下降，而替代方案需重新训练编码器。

Method: 发现向量位于单位球面并集中在5-6维流形上；对k-means质心进行归一化以修正MaxSim评分低估；引入GLIE框架，使用少量学习到的归一化质心作为轻量级索引和基，查询时仅搜索这些向量，并对顶级候选者解码重建完整嵌入以精确重排序。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1数据集上，每页4个向量即可保留未压缩系统80%的nDCG@5性能（优于先前最佳后处理方法的70%）；仅需31.5万参数且在千页训练集上耗时不到三分钟GPU时间；相比同等训练预算下的编码器微调，GLIE表现更优且适用于多种编码器和数据集。

Conclusion: GLIE通过按需重建证据而非采样，开辟了一条存储高效的检索新路径，其核心设计空间在于解码器，证明了利用几何特性进行高效检索的有效性。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrieval is the state-of-the-art for visual document search, but it pays for its accuracy in storage. Existing compression methods retain a subset or local average of the N~1,000 vectors per page. Under aggressive storage budgets, however, these methods degrade sharply, and alternatives require retraining the encoder. Investigating this degradation across three encoders, we found two consistent properties: the vectors lie exactly on the unit sphere and concentrate near a manifold of intrinsic dimension five to six. This geometry yields two insights. First, standard k-means centroids fall inside the sphere, causing systematic underestimation of MaxSim scores. Normalizing them to the surface is a free correction worth up to +0.093 nDCG@5 over raw centroids. Second, because the page manifold has few degrees of freedom, the full set of vectors can be regenerated from only a few. To this end, we introduce Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings (GLIE): k << N vectors per page learned from the normalized centroids to serve as both a lightweight index and a basis for regenerating the page's full embedding set. At query time, search runs exclusively on these k vectors, and a decoder expands only the top candidates back to all N vectors for exact rescoring. At four vectors per page on ViDoRe v1, GLIE retains nearly 80% of the uncompressed system's nDCG@5, against 70% for the best prior post-hoc method. These results use a 415K-parameter network fitted in under three GPU-minutes on just a thousand training pages. At a matched training budget, fine-tuning the encoder does not reach even the training-free stage of GLIE, and the full system beats it at every budget. These patterns hold across a second encoder and ViDoRe v2. By reconstructing evidence on demand rather than sampling it, GLIE opens a new axis for storage-efficient retrieval, with the decoder as its main design surface.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率焦点搜索（PFS），通过平衡启发式引导与下界推进，在焦点集合 admission 延迟时显著减少节点扩展次数；其 anytime 变体 APFS 在 GCTSP 上表现最优，且该机制可迁移至动态势搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统焦点搜索（FS）采用确定性策略，可能导致最小f值（f_min）长时间不变，从而阻碍 FOCAL 集合的更新和有效节点的纳入，限制了搜索效率。

Method: 引入概率焦点搜索（PFS），以概率 p 遵循 FS 的启发式选择，以概率 1-p 扩展 OPEN 表中 f 值最小的节点，以此促进下界提升并扩大 FOCAL 集合；同时将其应用于动态势搜索得到 PDPS，并在 N-Puzzle、TSP 等基准测试中验证。

Result: 在 f_min 出现长平台期导致 FOCAL admission 受阻的场景下（如 N-Puzzle 和 TSP），PFS 可减少约 90% 以上的节点扩展；APFS 在广义覆盖旅行商问题（GCTSP）上优于所有对比算法；但在确定性搜索本身高效的场景（如 Pancake Sorting）中收益较小。

Conclusion: 概率化机制能有效解决焦点搜索中因下界停滞导致的瓶颈问题，显著提升特定场景下的搜索效率，且具有良好的跨算法迁移能力。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS). We benchmark PFS against FS on N-Puzzle, Pancake Sorting, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP), and evaluate its anytime extension on the Generalized Covering TSP (GCTSP), using multiple $w$ and $p$ values. Across these benchmarks, the largest gains occur when long $f_{\min}$ plateaus delay useful FOCAL admissions; in such settings, the probabilistic factor may reduce node expansions by about 90\% or more (e.g., on N-Puzzle and TSP). For the anytime algorithm family, Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search (APFS) outperforms all tested algorithms in evaluating anytime methods on GCTSP. We also observe that the benefit is smaller when the deterministic search already advances efficiently (e.g., Pancake Sorting), indicating that the probabilistic factor is most useful when FOCAL admission is a search bottleneck. The PDPS transfer shows that the mechanism also transfers to potential guidance, although its common-success effects remain domain- and bound-dependent.

</details>


### [144] [Automating Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) Formulation Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10629)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的多智能体框架，能将自然语言问题描述自动转化为QUBO公式，并引入了包含100个问题的QUBOBench基准测试，实验显示该方法准确率达到68%，显著优于单调用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言描述转化为正确的二次无约束二进制优化（QUBO） formulations 过程耗时且需要大量领域专业知识，目前缺乏自动化解决方案。

Method: 构建了一个端到端的多智能体框架，支持从自然语言及结构化/非结构化测试用例自动生成QUBO公式；同时发布了QUBOBench基准数据集，涵盖12个应用领域的100个组合优化问题。

Result: 在QUBOBench基准上实现了68%的准确率，比直接单次调用的基线方法高出22%；分析表明“迭代自修复”机制是提升性能的关键组件。

Conclusion: 该多智能体框架有效解决了QUBO建模的自动化难题，通过迭代自修复机制显著提升了生成质量，相关数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) is a central formulation for combinatorial optimization and has gained increasing attention due to its compatibility with quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and quantum-inspired solvers. However, translating natural-language problem descriptions into correct QUBO formulations remains difficult, requiring the identification of binary variables, constraints, objective functions, penalty terms, and suitable penalty weights. This process is time-consuming and often demands substantial domain expertise. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end multi-agent framework that automatically generates QUBO formulations from natural-language problem descriptions, supported by structured or unstructured test cases.
